+    print("  T-063: MUST-FIX regression guards (untracked pseudo-unique, symlink containment escape)")
+
+    # T-063 MUST-FIX 1 counterexample: untracked_pseudo_unique. A tracked file and a
+    # genuinely-existing, non-gitignored *untracked* file share the same suffix. The
+    # narrowed (tracked-only) index from the prior rework made the tracked file look
+    # like the *only* candidate -- a false uniqueness born from excluding real,
+    # non-ignored worktree files rather than from a hardcoded denylist this time.
+    # codex T-063 repro: before_add resolves (bug), after_add is dangling (accidental
+    # fix-by-git-add, not by design). Post-fix must be dangling in BOTH states, since
+    # the untracked file is a real, non-ignored worktree fact independent of whether
+    # it has been `git add`ed yet.
+    untracked_root = REPO_ROOT / ".tmp" / f"verify-fixture-untracked-{uuid.uuid4().hex}"
+    round_dir7 = untracked_root / ".harnessloop" / "goals" / "20260108-001-fixture" / "rounds" / "0001"
+    try:
+        (round_dir7 / "evidence").mkdir(parents=True)
+        (round_dir7 / "reviews").mkdir(parents=True)
+        (round_dir7 / "scope-lock.md").write_text(
+            "# Scope Lock\n\n## Allowed Changes\n\n"
+            "- Write evidence under `rounds/0001/evidence/`.\n"
+            "- Write reviews under `rounds/0001/reviews/`.\n",
+            encoding="utf-8",
+        )
+        (untracked_root / "src" / "pkg").mkdir(parents=True)
+        (untracked_root / "src" / "pkg" / "real.md").write_text("tracked\n", encoding="utf-8")
+        (untracked_root / "scratch" / "pkg").mkdir(parents=True)
+        (untracked_root / "scratch" / "pkg" / "real.md").write_text("untracked, not ignored\n", encoding="utf-8")
+
+        (round_dir7 / "reviews" / "untracked.md").write_text(
+            "Ambiguous suffix -- one tracked file (src/pkg/real.md) and one genuinely "
+            "real, non-ignored but untracked file (scratch/pkg/real.md) share this "
+            "suffix, must NOT resolve:\n"
+            "- `pkg/real.md`\n",
+            encoding="utf-8",
+        )
+
+        git_available = shutil.which("git") is not None
+        if git_available:
+            init = subprocess.run(["git", "init", "-q"], cwd=untracked_root, capture_output=True)
+            add_tracked = subprocess.run(
+                ["git", "add", "src/pkg/real.md"], cwd=untracked_root, capture_output=True
+            )
+            git_available = init.returncode == 0 and add_tracked.returncode == 0
+
+        if git_available:
+            # Sanity: the tracked-only index (the prior rework's universe) sees only
+            # ONE candidate for this suffix -- proving the ambiguity below is surfaced
+            # by including untracked-not-ignored files, not by some other mechanism.
+            tracked_only_listed = subprocess.run(
+                ["git", "-C", str(untracked_root), "ls-files", "-z", "--cached", "--recurse-submodules"],
+                capture_output=True,
+            )
+            tracked_only_index: dict[str, list[tuple]] = {}
+            for raw in tracked_only_listed.stdout.split(b"\0"):
+                if not raw:
+                    continue
+                rel = raw.decode("utf-8")
+                parts = tuple(p for p in rel.split("/") if p)
+                if parts:
+                    tracked_only_index.setdefault(parts[-1], []).append(parts)
+            tracked_only_matches = [
+                c for c in tracked_only_index.get("real.md", [])
+                if len(c) >= 2 and c[-2:] == ("pkg", "real.md")
+            ]
+            check(
+                len(tracked_only_matches) == 1,
+                "mutation control: a tracked-files-only index sees only one `pkg/real.md` "
+                "candidate before scratch/pkg/real.md is git-added (proves the untracked/"
+                "not-ignored inclusion, not something else, is what surfaces the ambiguity "
+                "below; T-063 MUST-FIX 1: untracked_pseudo_unique)",
+            )
+
+            violations_before, _cov = verify_protocol.verify_project(untracked_root)
+            check(
+                any("pkg/real.md" in v["detail"] for v in violations_before),
+                "verify reports `pkg/real.md` as dangling (multiply-resolvable) while "
+                "scratch/pkg/real.md is still untracked-but-not-ignored, instead of "
+                "treating the tracked file as a false-unique match (T-063 MUST-FIX 1: "
+                "untracked_pseudo_unique, codex T-063)",
+            )
+
+            add_untracked = subprocess.run(
+                ["git", "add", "scratch/pkg/real.md"], cwd=untracked_root, capture_output=True
+            )
+            if add_untracked.returncode == 0:
+                violations_after, _cov = verify_protocol.verify_project(untracked_root)
+                check(
+                    any("pkg/real.md" in v["detail"] for v in violations_after),
+                    "verify still reports `pkg/real.md` as dangling after scratch/pkg/real.md "
+                    "is git-added too -- the same worktree fact must not flip the verdict "
+                    "purely because of index status (no regression once both are tracked)",
+                )
+        else:
+            print("  (skipped: git unavailable -- untracked_pseudo_unique counterexample)")
+    finally:
+        shutil.rmtree(untracked_root, ignore_errors=True)
+
+    # T-063 MUST-FIX 2 counterexample: symlink_containment_escape. A project-internal
+    # symlink whose *path* is inside the project but whose *target* resolves outside
+    # it. Lexical `os.path.normpath` containment alone passes all three resolution
+    # paths (direct base, .gitmodules base, suffix-index hit); canonical
+    # (`Path.resolve`) containment on both sides must reject all three.
+    if hasattr(os, "symlink"):
+        symlink_root = REPO_ROOT / ".tmp" / f"verify-fixture-symlink-{uuid.uuid4().hex}"
+        symlink_project = symlink_root / "project"
+        symlink_outside = symlink_root / "outside"
+        round_dir8 = symlink_project / ".harnessloop" / "goals" / "20260109-001-fixture" / "rounds" / "0001"
+        try:
+            (round_dir8 / "evidence").mkdir(parents=True)
+            (round_dir8 / "reviews").mkdir(parents=True)
+            (round_dir8 / "scope-lock.md").write_text(
+                "# Scope Lock\n\n## Allowed Changes\n\n"
+                "- Write evidence under `rounds/0001/evidence/`.\n"
+                "- Write reviews under `rounds/0001/reviews/`.\n",
+                encoding="utf-8",
+            )
+            (symlink_outside / "pkg").mkdir(parents=True)
+            (symlink_outside / "pkg" / "ghost.py").write_text("# outside the project\n", encoding="utf-8")
+            (symlink_outside / "pkg" / "external.md").write_text("# outside the project\n", encoding="utf-8")
+
+            symlinks_supported = True
+            try:
+                (symlink_project / "link").symlink_to(symlink_outside, target_is_directory=True)
+                (symlink_project / "deep" / "pkg").mkdir(parents=True)
+                (symlink_project / "deep" / "pkg" / "external.md").symlink_to(
+                    symlink_outside / "pkg" / "external.md"
+                )
+            except (OSError, NotImplementedError):
+                symlinks_supported = False
+
+            if symlinks_supported:
+                (symlink_project / ".gitmodules").write_text(
+                    '[submodule "x"]\n\tpath = link\n\turl = https://example.invalid/x.git\n',
+                    encoding="utf-8",
+                )
+                (round_dir8 / "reviews" / "symlink.md").write_text(
+                    "Direct base: project-internal symlink `link` points outside the project, "
+                    "must NOT resolve:\n"
+                    "- `link/pkg/ghost.py`\n"
+                    "\n"
+                    ".gitmodules base: `path = link` names the same escaping symlink, must "
+                    "also NOT resolve:\n"
+                    "- `pkg/ghost.py`\n"
+                    "\n"
+                    "Suffix fallback: a project-internal *file* symlink whose target lives "
+                    "outside the project, must NOT resolve:\n"
+                    "- `pkg/external.md`\n",
+                    encoding="utf-8",
+                )
+
+                git_available = shutil.which("git") is not None
+                if git_available:
+                    init = subprocess.run(["git", "init", "-q"], cwd=symlink_project, capture_output=True)
+                    add = subprocess.run(["git", "add", "-A"], cwd=symlink_project, capture_output=True)
+                    git_available = init.returncode == 0 and add.returncode == 0
+
+                if git_available:
+                    # -- direct base (1/3) --
+                    escaping_candidate = Path(
+                        os.path.normpath(str(symlink_project / "link/pkg/ghost.py"))
+                    )
+                    check(
+                        verify_protocol.is_under(escaping_candidate, symlink_project),
+                        "mutation control: lexical normpath containment alone WOULD accept "
+                        "`link/pkg/ghost.py` (its path is inside the project) -- proves the "
+                        "rejection below comes from canonical resolution, not lexical "
+                        "normpath (T-063 MUST-FIX 2: symlink_containment_escape, direct base)",
+                    )
+                    check(
+                        verify_protocol._resolve_in_project(
+                            symlink_project, "link/pkg/ghost.py", symlink_project
+                        )
+                        is None,
+                        "_resolve_in_project rejects a citation resolving through a "
+                        "project-internal symlink whose target is outside the project "
+                        "(T-063 MUST-FIX 2: symlink_containment_escape, direct base)",
+                    )
+
+                    # -- .gitmodules base (2/3) --
+                    roots = verify_protocol.submodule_roots(symlink_project)
+                    check(
+                        all(
+                            verify_protocol._canonical(r) != verify_protocol._canonical(symlink_outside)
+                            for r in roots
+                        ),
+                        "submodule_roots rejects a `.gitmodules` `path =` entry naming a "
+                        "project-internal symlink whose target is outside the project "
+                        "(T-063 MUST-FIX 2: symlink_containment_escape, .gitmodules base)",
+                    )
+
+                    # -- suffix fallback (3/3) --
+                    symlink_index = verify_protocol.build_suffix_index(symlink_project)
+                    escaping_match_path = symlink_project / "deep" / "pkg" / "external.md"
+                    check(
+                        verify_protocol.is_under(escaping_match_path, symlink_project),

+                        "the suffix-index hit `deep/pkg/external.md` (its path is inside the "

+                        "is a project-internal symlink pointing outside the project "

+                        "mutation control: both escaping symlink targets genuinely resolve to "
+                        "real files on disk (proves containment, not absence, drives the "
+                        "rejections above)",
+                    )
+                else:
+                    print("  (skipped: git unavailable -- symlink_containment_escape counterexample)")
+            else:
+                print("  (skipped: symlinks unsupported on this filesystem -- symlink_containment_escape counterexample)")

        f=base/rel; f.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True); f.write_text('fresh')
    for base in (mid_checkout,leaf_checkout):
        f=base/'ignored/pkg/ignored.md'; f.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True); f.write_text('ignored')
    nidx=new.build_suffix_index(outer)
    nested_cmd=run(outer,'git','submodule','foreach','--recursive','-q','printf \"%s|%s\\\\n\" \""'$name" "$displaypath"'"',check=False)

      'foreach_rc':nested_cmd.returncode,'foreach_paths':nested_cmd.stdout.splitlines(),
      'tracked_hits':suffix_hits(nidx,'pkg/tracked.md'),
      'middle_untracked':suffix_hits(nidx,'pkg/middle.md'),

      'newline_untracked':['/'.join(x) for x in nidx.get('line\\nbreak.md',[])],
      'ignored_hits':['/'.join(x) for x in nidx.get('ignored.md',[])]

      'reenter_resolve':str((p/'reenter/pkg/in.md').resolve()),
      'reenter_accepted':new._resolve_in_project(p,'reenter/pkg/in.md',p) is not None,
      'submodule_roots':[x.name for x in new.submodule_roots(p)]
    }

    raw=p/'link/../escape.md'; helper=new._resolve_in_project(p,'link/../escape.md',p)

    (p/'smod').symlink_to(outside/'sub',target_is_directory=True); (p/'.gitmodules').write_text('[submodule \"x\"]\\n\\tpath = smod/../mod\\n')

finally:
    shutil.rmtree(root,ignore_errors=True)
print(json.dumps(out,indent=2,ensure_ascii=False))

 succeeded in 321ms:
usage: verify_protocol.py [-h] [--project PROJECT] [--json]

Verify mechanical Harnessloop protocol gates (scope-lock containment and
citation existence). Citation spans that are regex/glob patterns, bare-domain
URLs (docs.python.org/..., github.com/...), or templated paths with an angle-
bracket placeholder (goals/<id>/...) are exempt automatically; a citation
known to be intentional prose rather than a real reference can be exempted
explicitly by putting '<!-- verify:ignore -->' on the same line or the line
before it. Citations are resolved against the project root, the round's goal
and round directories, the project's own .harnessloop/ directory (for
citations using a PATHISH_PREFIXES prefix verbatim, e.g. setup/data-
sources.md), and the root of every git submodule (any depth) declared in the
project's .gitmodules (canonical-containment-checked: a path or .gitmodules
entry that would resolve outside the project — including via a project-
internal symlink — is never treated as resolved). A trailing :<line>,
:<start>-<end>, or ::<anchor> locator is stripped before checking. If still
unresolved, a citation with >=2 path segments that matches exactly one file in
the project's tracked-plus-untracked-not-ignored worktree index (or, outside a
git working tree, the walked and noise-pruned tree) as a path suffix, and
whose matched path still actually exists, is also accepted. A citation ending
in / must resolve to a directory. Home-relative (~/...), filesystem-absolute
(/...), and Windows-absolute (C:/..., \\server\share...) citations are exempt
(out of project scope; counted in the citations_exempt_external coverage
field).

optional arguments:
  -h, --help            show this help message and exit
  --project PROJECT, -p PROJECT
                        Target project directory. Defaults to current
                        directory.
  --json                Print machine-readable JSON output.
    42	  Before existence is checked, a trailing locator suffix is stripped from
    43	  the citation: `:<line>`, `:<start>-<end>`, or `::<anchor>` (see
    44	  `strip_locator_suffix`). Reviewers routinely cite `path/to/file.py:123`
    45	  or `doc.md::some-anchor` — the locator addresses a position *within* an
    46	  already-real file, it is not part of the file's path, and checking the
    47	  literal locator-suffixed string against the filesystem always fails.
    48	  Both the original and the stripped form are tried, in that order, so a
    49	  citation that happens to end in something that is coincidentally not a
    50	  locator is not silently mis-resolved.
    51	
    52	  Every base resolution (project root, goal/round dirs, `.harnessloop/`,
    53	  submodule roots, and the suffix fallback below) is *containment-checked*
    54	  using canonical (symlink-resolved) paths on both sides, not merely a
    55	  lexical `normpath` comparison: the candidate and the project root are
    56	  each passed through `Path.resolve(strict=False)` before the containment
    57	  test, and the candidate must land inside the resolved project root (see
    58	  `_is_contained`). A citation containing `../` that would otherwise walk
    59	  outside the project (e.g. `../outside/ghost.py`), a `.gitmodules`
    60	  `path =` entry that points outside the project root, or a
    61	  project-internal symlink (a submodule-root path itself, an explicit-base
    62	  candidate, or a suffix-index hit) whose real target lives outside the
    63	  project tree, is never treated as resolved — even if something of that
    64	  name happens to exist on the host filesystem just outside the project
    65	  tree (TH-0008 REWORK: `submodule_parent_escape`; T-063 MUST-FIX 2:
    66	  `symlink_containment_escape` — lexical `normpath` containment alone
    67	  passes a symlink whose path is inside the project but whose target is
    68	  not, and that held across all three of the base-resolution, submodule-root,
    69	  and suffix-match paths, not just one). Resolving the project root too
    70	  (not only the candidate) matters even absent any project-authored
    71	  symlink: on macOS, e.g., `/tmp` is itself a symlink to `/private/tmp`, so
    72	  a project rooted under `/tmp` would otherwise be compared against an
    73	  unresolved parent and see spurious mismatches. A broken symlink's
    74	  `resolve(strict=False)` does not raise — it normalizes past the point the
    75	  target stops existing — so containment alone does not double as an
    76	  existence check; the existing match-time re-verification (`_exists_as`,
    77	  `broken_symlink` / `stale_index_after_delete`) still owns that job and is
    78	  always applied in addition to, not instead of, containment.
    79	
    80	  If a citation still does not resolve against any of the bases above
    81	  (after locator-stripping), it is tried once more as a path *suffix*
    82	  against the project's indexed files (see `build_suffix_index` /
    83	  `suffix_unique_match`): this is the fallback for a citation that is
    84	  correct but written relative to neither the project root, the
    85	  round/goal directory, `.harnessloop/`, nor any submodule root — e.g.
    86	  `harnessloop-setup/SKILL.md` for a file that actually lives at
    87	  `plugins/harnessloop/skills/harnessloop-setup/SKILL.md`. This fallback is
    88	  deliberately conservative: it requires at least two path segments (a
    89	  single bare filename never qualifies — that shape is both the most
    90	  common typo and the least informative citation), compares whole path
    91	  *segments* rather than raw string suffixes (so `os.html` cannot match
    92	  `macos.html`), and only exempts the citation when exactly one indexed
    93	  file matches that suffix *and* the specific path that match names still
    94	  actually exists (or, for a citation ending in `/`, is still actually a
    95	  directory) at the moment of checking — zero or multiple matches both
    96	  still report `dangling-citation`, and so does a unique match whose
    97	  target has since disappeared or was never real to begin with (a broken
    98	  symlink that `os.walk` lists in `filenames` without following it, or an
    99	  index entry for a file deleted after the index was built but before this
   100	  citation was checked; TH-0008 REWORK: `broken_symlink`,
   101	  `stale_index_after_delete`).
   102	
   103	  The index backing this fallback is built from every file that genuinely
   104	  exists in the project's worktree and is not gitignored — tracked *and*
   105	  untracked-but-not-ignored (`git ls-files -z --cached --recurse-submodules`
   106	  plus `git ls-files -z --others --exclude-standard`, the latter extended
   107	  into nested submodules via `git submodule foreach --recursive` since
   108	  `--others` itself has no `--recurse-submodules` mode — run from the
   109	  project root, when the project root is itself a git working-tree top
   110	  level) — falling back to walking the filesystem (pruning
   111	  `NOISE_DIR_NAMES`) only when the project is not a git working-tree root or
   112	  `git` is unavailable. "Unique" means unique among this real-worktree,
   113	  non-ignored surface, not literally "git-tracked": an earlier rework
   114	  narrowed the index to tracked files only, which closed the noise-pruning
   115	  blind spot below but opened a new one — a round's own freshly-produced
   116	  evidence/review files are untracked by construction, so a genuine
   117	  same-suffix collision between a tracked file and such a fresh untracked
   118	  one was invisible to the ambiguity check, making a citation that should
   119	  have been multiply-resolvable look uniquely resolved instead (T-063
   120	  MUST-FIX 1: `untracked_pseudo_unique`). A gitignored file colliding with a
   121	  tracked/untracked file's suffix is still invisible to the index either
   122	  way (that generated-output exclusion is the point of `--exclude-standard`),
   123	  and — unlike the prior walk-based index — a real, non-ignored source
   124	  directory that happens to share a name with an entry in `NOISE_DIR_NAMES`
   125	  (e.g. a project that genuinely has a source directory named `build/`) is
   126	  indexed and can correctly participate in an ambiguity instead of being
   127	  silently pruned out of consideration (TH-0008 REWORK:
   128	  `noise_pruned_ambiguity`). The walk-based fallback, when it is used, still
   129	  prunes `NOISE_DIR_NAMES` and is subject to the same pruning blind spot it
   130	  always was.
   131	
   132	  A citation ending in `/` names a directory, not a file: it only resolves
   133	  when the matched filesystem entry is actually a directory (`is_dir()`),
   134	  never when it merely shares a path with an existing file of the same
   135	  name (TH-0008 REWORK: `trailing_slash_file` — `pkg/real.md/` must not
   136	  resolve against the file `pkg/real.md`). This directory semantics is
   137	  applied uniformly to explicit-base resolution and to the suffix
   138	  fallback.
   139	
   140	  A citation that starts with `~/` (home-relative), `/` (POSIX filesystem
   141	  absolute), or a Windows-style absolute path — a drive letter
   142	  (`C:/...`, written `C:\\...` before backslashes are normalized to `/`)
   143	  or a UNC path (`\\\\server\\share\\...`, normalized to `//server/share/...`,
   144	  which already starts with `/` and so falls under the same check) — is
   145	  exempt from existence checking entirely, for the same reason a
   146	  `scheme://` URL is exempt: it names a location outside the project tree,
   147	  and Rule B has no project-declared base to resolve it against —
   148	  inventing one (e.g. treating `/` as the OS root, or `C:/` as a Windows
   149	  drive root) would let the check silently walk outside the project, or
   150	  behave differently depending on which OS the check happens to run on.
   151	  This is a real, known gap: a review that cites a path inside an external
   152	  design wiki kept outside this project (e.g. under a user's
   153	  `~/.llm-wiki/...`) is never verified, because harnessloop has no way to
   154	  know where — or whether — that external tree exists. Closing that gap
   155	  requires a project to declare an additional resolution base for such
   156	  external stores; that is a separate, project-level protocol decision and
   157	  is out of scope here. Unlike before, this exemption is no longer silent:
   158	  every span skipped for this reason is counted in the `coverage` dict's
   159	  `citations_exempt_external` field (see `_empty_coverage`), so a run's
   160	  coverage line makes visible how much of its citation surface was waved
   161	  through unchecked rather than hiding that number entirely.
   162	
   163	  Two honesty notes on what this rule cannot mechanically guarantee even
   164	  after the above:
   165	
   166	  - Case sensitivity of the final existence check is whatever the host
   167	    filesystem gives it, not a Rule B decision: `Path.exists()` /
   168	    `is_dir()` are case-sensitive on a typical Linux filesystem and
   169	    case-insensitive (but case-preserving) by default on macOS and
   170	    Windows. A citation with the wrong case for a real file can therefore
   171	    pass or fail depending on which machine runs the check — this rule
   172	    does not normalize case itself and cannot make that determinism
   173	    project-wide.
   174	  - The suffix-unique fallback proves "exactly one indexed file has this
   175	    suffix", not "this is the file the reviewer meant to cite". A
   176	    reviewer who mistypes `mistyped/config.yaml` when they meant a
   177	    different file will still get a pass if `vendor/mistyped/config.yaml`
   178	    happens to be the tree's only file ending in `mistyped/config.yaml` —
   179	    the algorithm cannot distinguish a lucky coincidental match from the
   180	    intended one. This is inherent to any suffix-based fallback and is not
   181	    something this rework (or any purely mechanical suffix match) can
   182	    close; it is recorded here rather than fixed because fixing it would
   183	    require either dropping the fallback (reintroducing the false
   184	    positives it exists to remove) or requiring citations to be written
   185	    unambiguously (a review-authoring discipline change, not a Rule B one).
   186	
   187	Exit codes: 0 = pass, 1 = violations found, 2 = usage error.
   188	"""
   189	
   190	from __future__ import annotations
   534	def _git_tracked_index(project: Path) -> dict[str, list[tuple[str, ...]]] | None:
   535	    """Git worktree-file index for `build_suffix_index`, or `None` if
   536	    `project` is not itself a git working-tree root (or `git` is
   537	    unavailable).
   538	
   539	    Only engages when `project` *is* the git top level — not merely inside
   540	    one — so a scratch directory nested under some ancestor repository
   541	    (e.g. a test fixture created under this repo's own working tree without
   542	    being `git add`ed) correctly falls back to the walk-based index instead
   543	    of silently seeing zero tracked files and treating every suffix lookup
   544	    as absent.
   545	
   546	    The uniqueness universe is every file that genuinely exists in the
   547	    worktree and is not gitignored — tracked *and* untracked-but-not-ignored
   548	    — not merely `git ls-files`'s tracked set. `git ls-files -z --cached
   549	    --recurse-submodules` supplies the tracked half (including nested
   550	    submodules, any depth); `git ls-files -z --others --exclude-standard`
   551	    supplies untracked-but-not-ignored files in the top-level project (`git`
   552	    has no `--recurse-submodules` support for `--others` — see `git
   553	    help ls-files` — so nested submodules' own untracked files are collected
   554	    separately via `git submodule foreach --recursive`, prefixed with each
   555	    submodule's path). Restricting to tracked files alone (TH-0008 REWORK
   556	    original fix) traded one false-negative for another: a round's just-produced
   557	    evidence/review files are untracked by construction (nothing has `git
   558	    add`ed them yet), so a genuine same-suffix collision between a tracked
   559	    file and such a fresh untracked one was invisible to the ambiguity check
   560	    and a citation that should have been flagged as multiply-resolvable
   561	    instead looked uniquely resolved (T-063 MUST-FIX 1:
   562	    `untracked_pseudo_unique`). Gitignored output (`node_modules/`, `dist/`,
   563	    `build/` artifacts, `.venv/`, ...) is still excluded by `--exclude-standard`,
   564	    so this keeps the benefit of not needing a hardcoded noise-directory
   565	    denylist (see module docstring: "unique" now means unique among every
   566	    real, non-ignored file in the worktree, not literally "git-tracked" nor
   567	    the whole tree including ignored output).
   568	    """
   569	    try:
   570	        toplevel = subprocess.run(
   571	            ["git", "-C", str(project), "rev-parse", "--show-toplevel"],
   572	            capture_output=True,
   573	            text=True,
   574	            timeout=10,
   575	        )
   576	    except (OSError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired):
   577	        return None
   578	    if toplevel.returncode != 0:
   579	        return None
   580	    top = toplevel.stdout.strip()
   581	    if not top:
   582	        return None
   583	    try:
   584	        if Path(top).resolve() != project.resolve():
   585	            return None
   586	    except OSError:
   587	        return None
   588	
   589	    try:
   590	        tracked = subprocess.run(
   591	            ["git", "-C", str(project), "ls-files", "-z", "--cached", "--recurse-submodules"],
   592	            capture_output=True,
   593	            timeout=60,
   594	        )
   595	    except (OSError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired):
   596	        return None
   597	    if tracked.returncode != 0:
   598	        return None
   599	
   600	    raw_entries: list[bytes] = [seg for seg in tracked.stdout.split(b"\0") if seg]
   601	
   602	    try:
   603	        untracked = subprocess.run(
   604	            ["git", "-C", str(project), "ls-files", "-z", "--others", "--exclude-standard"],
   605	            capture_output=True,
   606	            timeout=60,
   607	        )
   608	    except (OSError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired):
   609	        untracked = None
   610	    if untracked is not None and untracked.returncode == 0:
   611	        raw_entries.extend(seg for seg in untracked.stdout.split(b"\0") if seg)
   612	
   613	    # Nested submodules' own untracked-but-not-ignored files: `--others` has no
   614	    # `--recurse-submodules` mode, so shell out to `submodule foreach --recursive`
   615	    # and run the same lookup inside each, prefixing with $displaypath (the
   616	    # submodule's path relative to `project`). A read loop keyed on NUL, not
   617	    # newline, keeps this safe for filenames containing embedded newlines, and
   618	    # `printf ... \0` re-delimits with NUL so the outer split below still works.
   619	    try:
   620	        nested = subprocess.run(
   621	            [
   622	                "git", "-C", str(project), "submodule", "foreach", "--recursive", "-q",
   623	                'git ls-files -z --others --exclude-standard | '
   624	                'while IFS= read -r -d "" f; do printf "%s/%s\\0" "$displaypath" "$f"; done',
   625	            ],
   626	            capture_output=True,
   627	            timeout=60,
   628	        )
   629	    except (OSError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired):
   630	        nested = None
   631	    if nested is not None and nested.returncode == 0:
   632	        raw_entries.extend(seg for seg in nested.stdout.split(b"\0") if seg)
   633	
   634	    index: dict[str, list[tuple[str, ...]]] = {}
   635	    seen: set[str] = set()
   636	    for raw in raw_entries:
   637	        try:
   638	            rel = raw.decode("utf-8")
   639	        except UnicodeDecodeError:
   640	            continue
   641	        if rel in seen:
   642	            continue
   643	        seen.add(rel)
   644	        parts = tuple(p for p in rel.split("/") if p)
   645	        if not parts:
   646	            continue
   647	        index.setdefault(parts[-1], []).append(parts)
   648	    return index
   649	
   650	
   651	def build_suffix_index(project: Path) -> dict[str, list[tuple[str, ...]]]:
   652	    """Index files under `project` by basename, once per run.
   653	
   654	    Backs the Rule B suffix-unique fallback (`suffix_unique_match`): after a
   655	    citation fails to resolve against every explicit base (project root,
   656	    goal/round dirs, `.harnessloop/`, submodule roots — with and without a
   657	    locator suffix stripped), it is tried once more as a path *suffix*
   658	    against this index. Building it once per `verify_project` call (rather
   659	    than walking the tree per citation) keeps that fallback from turning an
   660	    O(citations) check into an O(citations * tree size) one.
   661	
   662	    Primary source is the tracked-plus-untracked-not-ignored worktree index
   663	    (`_git_tracked_index`, see its docstring), used whenever `project` is
   664	    itself a git working-tree root. Falls back to
   665	    walking the filesystem directly — `os.walk` with in-place `dirnames`
   666	    pruning of `NOISE_DIR_NAMES` (`.git`, `node_modules`, build output,
   667	    venvs, ...), not `Path.rglob("*")`, since a project with vendored
   668	    dependencies can have orders of magnitude more files inside those
   669	    directories than in the rest of the tree — when `project` is not a git
   670	    working-tree root or `git` is unavailable. Each entry maps a basename to
   671	    the list of path-segment tuples (relative to `project`) of every
   672	    indexed file with that basename. Note that an indexed entry is not, by
   673	    itself, proof the file still exists: `suffix_unique_match` re-verifies
   674	    the specific matched path against the real filesystem before accepting
   675	    it (see its docstring).
   676	    """
   677	    tracked = _git_tracked_index(project)
   678	    if tracked is not None:
   679	        return tracked
   680	
   681	    index: dict[str, list[tuple[str, ...]]] = {}
   682	    for dirpath, dirnames, filenames in os.walk(project):
   683	        dirnames[:] = [d for d in dirnames if d not in NOISE_DIR_NAMES]
   684	        rel_dir = Path(dirpath).relative_to(project)
   685	        dir_parts = () if str(rel_dir) == "." else rel_dir.parts
   686	        for filename in filenames:
   687	            parts = dir_parts + (filename,)
   688	            index.setdefault(filename, []).append(parts)
   689	    return index
   690	
   706	def suffix_unique_match(
   707	    cleaned: str, index: dict[str, list[tuple[str, ...]]], project: Path
   708	) -> bool:
   709	    """True if `cleaned` matches exactly one indexed file as a path
   710	    *suffix*, and that specific file still actually exists.
   711	
   712	    Last-resort Rule B fallback (see module docstring) for a citation that
   713	    is correct but written relative to none of the explicit bases — e.g.
   714	    `harnessloop-setup/SKILL.md` for a file that actually lives at
   715	    `plugins/harnessloop/skills/harnessloop-setup/SKILL.md`. Deliberately
   716	    conservative in four ways, each guarding a specific false-negative
   717	    risk:
   718	
   719	    - Comparison is by path *segment*, not raw string suffix — `os.html`
   720	      must not match `.../macos.html` (string `endswith` would).
   721	    - At least two path segments are required — a single bare filename
   722	      (`agents/`-style single component citations, or a bare basename like
   723	      `verify_protocol.py`) is the shape most likely to be a typo and least
   724	      likely to disambiguate anything, so it never qualifies here; it is
   725	      still checked (and can still fail) against the explicit bases above.
   726	    - The match must be *unique* across the index — 0 hits is still
   727	      dangling (no such file), and >=2 hits is also still dangling (an
   728	      ambiguous suffix is not a resolved citation; picking one of several
   729	      candidates would silently paper over which file was meant).
   730	    - The unique match's specific path is re-checked against the real
   731	      filesystem before being accepted — `is_dir()` if `cleaned` ends in
   732	      `/`, otherwise `exists()` (`_exists_as`) — rather than trusted purely
   733	      because the index once recorded it. The index can otherwise be
   734	      stale (a file the index recorded can be deleted between
   735	      `build_suffix_index` and this call — TH-0008 REWORK:
   736	      `stale_index_after_delete`) or, when it was built by walking the
   737	      filesystem, can contain a broken symlink that `os.walk` lists in
   738	      `filenames` without ever following it (TH-0008 REWORK:
   739	      `broken_symlink`); either way, treating the indexed entry as
   740	      sufficient proof of existence would accept a citation to something
   741	      that, right now, does not resolve to a real file.
   742	
   743	    A fifth guard, added in T-063 MUST-FIX 2 (`symlink_containment_escape`):
   744	    the unique match's specific path is also *canonical-containment-checked*
   745	    (`_is_contained`) against `project` before being accepted, not merely
   746	    assumed safe because it came from the index. The index is built from
   747	    `git ls-files`, which lists a tracked symlink like any other tracked
   748	    entry — a citation ending in, say, `pkg/external.md` where
   749	    `<project>/deep/pkg/external.md` is itself a symlink pointing at a real
   750	    file *outside* the project would otherwise pass the existence check
   751	    (the symlink's target genuinely exists) while resolving to something
   752	    the project does not control. Containment is checked first and
   753	    existence second, deliberately not combined into one step: a broken
   754	    symlink still passes containment (its lexical path is inside the
   755	    project even though nothing exists at the far end) and is correctly
   756	    caught by the existence check that follows, exactly as before.
   757	    """
   758	    parts = tuple(p for p in cleaned.split("/") if p)
   759	    if len(parts) < 2:
   760	        return False
   761	    candidates = index.get(parts[-1], [])
   762	    matches = [c for c in candidates if len(c) >= len(parts) and c[-len(parts) :] == parts]
   763	    if len(matches) != 1:
   764	        return False
   765	    match_path = project.joinpath(*matches[0])
   766	    if not _is_contained(match_path, project):
   767	        return False
   768	    return _exists_as(match_path, cleaned.endswith("/"))
   769	
   770	
   771	def _resolve_in_project(base: Path, cited: str, project: Path) -> Path | None:
   772	    """Join `base` and `cited`, normalize, and return the result only if it
   773	    stays within `project` under *canonical* containment — otherwise `None`.
   774	
   775	    Guards against a citation containing `../` segments walking the join
   776	    outside the project tree, where `Path.exists()` would silently consult
   777	    the real host filesystem for an unrelated path that happens to share a
   778	    name with something the review meant to cite (TH-0008 REWORK:
   779	    `submodule_parent_escape` — the same containment discipline
   780	    `submodule_roots` applies to `.gitmodules` entries, applied here to
   781	    every citation resolution). Lexical `normpath` containment alone is not
   782	    enough for a project-internal symlink whose target lives outside the
   783	    project (e.g. `<project>/link -> /outside`, cited as `link/pkg/x.py`):
   784	    the joined-and-normalized candidate's *path* is lexically inside the
   785	    project even though what it resolves to is not (T-063 MUST-FIX 2:
   786	    `symlink_containment_escape`) — `_is_contained` resolves both sides
   787	    before comparing, so this is rejected too. The returned `candidate` is
   788	    still the lexical (non-canonical) join, not the resolved path: callers
   789	    only need it for the subsequent `_exists_as` check, which itself
   790	    follows symlinks via `Path.exists()` / `is_dir()`.
   791	    """
   792	    candidate = Path(os.path.normpath(str(base / cited)))
   793	    if not _is_contained(candidate, project):
   794	        return None
   795	    return candidate
   796	
   797	
   798	def _any_base_resolves(cited: str, bases: list[Path], project: Path, want_dir: bool) -> bool:
   799	    """True if `cited` resolves (containment-checked, directory-semantics-
   800	    aware) against any of `bases`."""
   801	    for base in bases:
   802	        resolved = _resolve_in_project(base, cited, project)
   803	        if resolved is not None and _exists_as(resolved, want_dir):
   804	            return True
   805	    return False
   806

+# T-064 · harnessloop v0.15.0 / T-063 MUST-FIX 确认审
+
+## Summary
+
+`1153b7f` 对 T-063 两个原始反例的字面复现均已闭合：untracked 冲突在 `git add` 前后都保持 dangling，普通 symlink 出界的直接 base、`.gitmodules` base、suffix hit 三路也都由假绿转为 dangling。真实 recursive-init 的二层 submodule untracked 覆盖完整，macOS `/tmp -> /private/tmp`、多级 symlink 和“经项目外中转但最终落回项目内”的正反例也符合预期。
+
+但不能确认 TH-0008 fixed：gitignored 的真实冲突仍制造与 T-063 同形、且会被 `git add -f` 翻转的伪唯一；canonical containment 在 `symlink/..` 组合上因先 `normpath` 后 `resolve` 而把真实项目外路径改写为项目内同名路径，直接 base 与 `.gitmodules` base 均可端到端假绿。此外，`--cached` 把已从工作树删除的 tracked 项仍计入“真实存在”宇宙，制造新的假阳性并使索引 docstring 不实。
+
+## Files touched
+
+- `.hopper/handoffs/T-064-output.md`：本只读确认审交付物。
+- 被评审的 `harnessloop` 源码：none；所有 Git/submodule/symlink fixture 均建于 `/tmp` 并已删除，结束时 `git -C harnessloop status --short` 无输出。
+
+## Acceptance verification (4/4)
+
+### 1. 两条 T-063 MUST-FIX 原始反例 — **字面反例均闭合**
+
+从 `git show 1153b7f^:plugins/harnessloop/skills/harnessloop-loop/scripts/verify_protocol.py` 加载 v0.14.0，从 `git show 1153b7f:...` 加载 v0.15.0，原样复跑 `.hopper/handoffs/T-063-output.md` 第 2、3 节 fixture，结果：
+
+```text
+untracked_pseudo_unique:
+  before git add scratch:
+    v0.14 dangling=[]
+    v0.15 dangling=["pkg/real.md"]
+    v0.15 suffix hits=["src/pkg/real.md","scratch/pkg/real.md"]
+  after git add scratch:
+    v0.14 dangling=["pkg/real.md"]
+    v0.15 dangling=["pkg/real.md"]
+
+symlink_containment_escape:
+  v0.14 helpers:
+    direct_candidate_accepted=true
+    submodule_roots=[".../project/link"]
+    suffix_unique_match=true
+    end_to_end_dangling=[]
+  v0.15 helpers:
+    direct_candidate_accepted=false
+    submodule_roots=[]
+    suffix_unique_match=false
+    end_to_end_dangling=[
+      "link/pkg/ghost.py",
+      "pkg/ghost.py",
+      "pkg/external.md"
+    ]
+  mutation targets:
+    .../outside/pkg/ghost.py
+    .../outside/pkg/ghost.py
+```
+
+对应实现调用点为 `verify_protocol.py:527`（`.gitmodules`）、`:765-768`（suffix hit）、`:792-795`（直接/任意 base）。新增官方守卫位于 `scripts/validate.py:1286-1539`，且基线全绿：
+
+```text
+$ HARNESSLOOP_SKIP_CLAUDE=1 python3 scripts/validate.py
+...
+ok: verify reports `pkg/real.md` as dangling ... untracked_pseudo_unique
+ok: _resolve_in_project rejects ... direct base
+ok: submodule_roots rejects ... .gitmodules base
+ok: suffix_unique_match rejects ... suffix fallback
+Plugin framework validation passed.
+```
+
+因此，“T-063 原始两个最小反例是否由修前假绿变为修后报错”这一窄问题答案是 **是**。
+
+### 2. 新唯一性宇宙 — **普通 untracked 与二层 submodule 正确；仍有 ignored 伪唯一和 stale-tracked 假阳性**
+
+实现确实使用三路 NUL 查询：tracked 为 `git ls-files -z --cached --recurse-submodules`（`verify_protocol.py:589-600`），顶层 untracked 为 `git ls-files -z --others --exclude-standard`（`:602-611`），已 checkout submodule 的 untracked 为 `git submodule foreach --recursive`（`:613-632`），最后按完整相对路径去重（`:634-648`）。
+
+真实 `outer -> modules/middle -> deps/leaf` fixture 执行 `git -c protocol.file.allow=always submodule update --init --recursive` 后：
+
+```text
+recursive_init_rc=0
+foreach:
+  modules/middle|modules/middle
+  deps/leaf|modules/middle/deps/leaf
+tracked pkg/tracked.md:
+  modules/middle/deps/leaf/deep/pkg/tracked.md
+  modules/middle/mid/pkg/tracked.md
+  top/pkg/tracked.md
+untracked:
+  modules/middle/fresh/pkg/middle.md
+  modules/middle/deps/leaf/fresh/pkg/leaf.md
+newline filename:
+  "modules/middle/deps/leaf/fresh/pkg/line\nbreak.md"
+ignored.md hits=[]
+```
+
+所以 recursive-init 后的嵌套 tracked/untracked 覆盖完整，NUL 边界也通过换行文件名实测；先前未 init 时 leaf 文件本身不在 worktree，不能算漏索引。
+
+非 Git 回退也仍与文档一致：
+
+```text
+_git_tracked_index(non_git_project) = None
+src/pkg/real.md exists
+build/pkg/real.md exists
+indexed hits=["src/pkg/real.md"]
+suffix_unique_match("pkg/real.md")=true
+```
+
+即 `os.walk` 在 `verify_protocol.py:681-689` 继续剪掉 `NOISE_DIR_NAMES`；module docstring `:128-130` 和 `build_suffix_index` docstring `:662-675` 如实登记了这个旧盲点。
+
+#### MUST-FIX A：gitignored 真实冲突仍是同形伪唯一
+
+`--exclude-standard` 在 `verify_protocol.py:603-605` 明确排除 ignored 文件。构造 tracked `src/pkg/real.md`、真实且 ignored 的 `scratch/pkg/real.md` 与引用 `pkg/real.md`：
+
+```text
+git check-ignore scratch/pkg/real.md -> rc 0
+before git add -f:
+  suffix hits=["src/pkg/real.md"]
+  dangling=[]
+after git add -f scratch/pkg/real.md:
+  suffix hits=["scratch/pkg/real.md","src/pkg/real.md"]
+  dangling=["pkg/real.md"]

+复现步骤：
 
-Output streaming to `T-064-output.log`. Status updates here.
+```bash
+T064_REPRO="$(mktemp -d /tmp/t064-ignore.XXXXXX)"
+git -C "$T064_REPRO" init -q

+PY
+```
+
+实测为 `before-force-add [...] True`、`after-force-add [...] False`。同一文件系统事实仅因 index 状态翻转，正是 T-063 判为 MUST-FIX 的核心性质；把边界从 “untracked” 平移到 “ignored” 并未消除该假阴性。module docstring `verify_protocol.py:120-122` 诚实披露 ignored 文件不可见，但披露不等于闭合。
+
+#### MUST-FIX B：已删除 tracked 项不属于“真实存在”宇宙，却仍参与唯一性
+
+`git ls-files --cached`（`verify_protocol.py:590-600`）会列出工作树中已删除但尚未从 index 删除的 tracked 文件。实现只在“索引恰好唯一”后复验该路径（`:761-768`）；一旦一个不存在项与一个真实项同后缀，`:763-764` 会先以 `len(matches) != 1` 返回，根本没有过滤不存在项：
+
+```text
+src/pkg/real.md exists=true
+gone/pkg/real.md exists=false
+git ls-files --deleted=["gone/pkg/real.md"]
+index hits=["gone/pkg/real.md","src/pkg/real.md"]
+suffix_unique_match=false
+end_to_end dangling=["pkg/real.md"]
+```
+
+这不是假阴性，而是 TH-0008 所要减少的假阳性；也直接反驳 module docstring `:103-112` 和 `_git_tracked_index` docstring `:546-567` 的 “every file that genuinely exists in the worktree”。
+
+### 3. canonical containment — **普通组合正确；`symlink/..` 可绕**
+
+先验证要求中的正常组合：
+
+```text
+multi-level escape:
+  project/hop1 -> project/hop2 -> outside
+  hop1/pkg/out.md resolves outside
+  _resolve_in_project(...) = None
+
+outside intermediary, final target inside:
+  project/reenter -> outside/return -> project/inside
+  reenter/pkg/in.md resolves inside
+  _resolve_in_project(...) accepted
+  .gitmodules roots=["reenter"]  # escaping hop1 was dropped
+
+project root under symlink:
+  project argument=/tmp/.../project-alias
+  project.resolve()=/private/tmp/.../project-real
+  _is_contained(alias/pkg/x.md, alias)=true
+  _resolve_in_project(...)=accepted
+```
+
+原始三路无 `..` escape 也确实分别在 `verify_protocol.py:527`、`:766`、`:793` 调用 `_is_contained`，并分别返回拒绝；suffix 路径对项目外 file symlink 保持安全。
+
+#### MUST-FIX C：先词法折叠、后 canonicalize 改变了 symlink/`..` 的真实路径语义
+
+`_is_contained` 本身在 `verify_protocol.py:277-309` 正确对两侧 `resolve(strict=False)`；但 `_resolve_in_project` 在把 candidate 交给它之前，先于 `:792` 执行 `os.path.normpath(base / cited)`。`normpath` 会把 `link/..` 纯词法消掉，而真实文件系统应先跟随 `link` 再处理 `..`。
+
+最小复现：
+
+```bash
+python3 - <<'PY'
+import importlib.util, shutil, tempfile
+from pathlib import Path
+s = Path("harnessloop/plugins/harnessloop/skills/harnessloop-loop/scripts/verify_protocol.py")
+sp = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("vp", s)
+vp = importlib.util.module_from_spec(sp); sp.loader.exec_module(vp)
+r = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="t064-order-", dir="/tmp"))
+try:
+    p, out = r/"project", r/"outside"
+    (out/"sub").mkdir(parents=True); (out/"escape.md").write_text("outside")
+    p.mkdir(); (p/"escape.md").write_text("coincidental inside")
+    (p/"link").symlink_to(out/"sub", target_is_directory=True)
+    raw = p/"link/../escape.md"
+    got = vp._resolve_in_project(p, "link/../escape.md", p)
+    print("raw_resolves_to", raw.resolve())
+    print("raw_contained", vp._is_contained(raw, p))
+    print("helper_returns", got)
+    print("helper_target", got.resolve() if got else None)
+finally:
+    shutil.rmtree(r)
+PY
+```
+
+实测：
+
+```text
+raw_resolves_to /private/tmp/.../outside/escape.md
+raw_contained False
+helper_returns /tmp/.../project/escape.md
+helper_target /private/tmp/.../project/escape.md
+```
+
+把引用 `link/../escape.md` 放进 review 后，v0.14 与 v0.15 均 `dangling=[]`。也就是说 v0.15 没有 containment-check 原始 candidate，而是先把它改写成另一个项目内同名文件后再检查。
+
+`.gitmodules` 有同一顺序错误：`submodule_roots` 先在 `verify_protocol.py:525-527` 对 candidate 做 `normpath` 后才 containment-check，却在 `:529-530` 对未折叠的原 candidate 做 `is_dir()` 并返回它。fixture：
+
+```text
+project/smod -> outside/sub
+.gitmodules path = smod/../mod
+raw .gitmodules path resolves to outside/mod
+_is_contained(raw_path, project)=false
+submodule_roots nevertheless returns [".../project/smod/../mod"]
+
+project/mod/pkg/modghost.md exists
+project/other/pkg/modghost.md exists
+suffix hits=2
+suffix_unique_match=false
+review cites pkg/modghost.md
+end_to_end dangling=[]   # 只能是错误接纳的 .gitmodules base 使其假绿
+```
+
+因此直接 base 与 `.gitmodules` base 都可独立绕过；suffix hit 这一条仍安全，但“三条路径统一受 canonical containment 保护”的整体声明不成立。
+
+### 4. 诚实性 — **索引边界与固有残留诚实；canonical 与“真实存在”两处过强**
+
+- canonical module docstring `verify_protocol.py:52-64` 声称 candidate 会直接经 `Path.resolve(strict=False)` 且项目外 symlink/`..` “never treated as resolved”；实际 `:792` 与 `:525-527` 会先词法折叠，以上反例假绿。`--help` 在 `:982-990` 同步了相同的绝对声明，因此同步了文字，但没有同步到真实能力。
+- 索引关于 “tracked + untracked-not-ignored” 的边界描述与查询一致（module docstring `:103-130`，helper docstring `:546-567`，`--help :987-990`），ignored 不可见也在 `:120-122` 明说。可是“every file that genuinely exists” 不准确：`:590-600` 纳入 deleted tracked，且多命中时不会做存在性过滤。
+- 非 Git 回退仍按 `:128-130,662-689` 的文字剪枝，实测一致。
+- 两条既有固有残留未被悄悄改称已修，登记仍在 `:163-185`。行为复验：
+
+```text
+only vendor/mistyped/config.yaml exists
+suffix_unique_match("mistyped/config.yaml")=true
+
+actual Case/File.md; citation case/file.md on this macOS volume
+Path.exists()=true
+_any_base_resolves(...)=true
+```
+
+后者正体现文档所述宿主大小写依赖，前者正体现后缀语义碰撞；两项均仍诚实。
+
+## Decisions / deviations
+
+- Verdict 使用任务专用枚举 `MUST-FIX`，而不是通用模板的 `REWORK`。
+- 判定口径：gitignored 文件虽可被排除为正向解析目标，但只要它真实存在并与一个可解析候选同后缀，就至少应阻止 suffix fallback 声称“唯一”；否则 `git add -f` 仍会翻转同一 worktree 事实。这是沿用 T-063 的保守假阴性口径。
+- T-063 原始两例与扩展反例分开结论：不抹杀原始修复已经生效，也不以原始守卫全绿代替组合审计。
+- 未修改被评审源码；全部临时 fixture 已清理。

+`1153b7f` 对 T-063 两个原始反例的字面复现均已闭合：untracked 冲突在 `git add` 前后都保持 dangling，普通 symlink 出界的直接 base、`.gitmodules` base、suffix hit 三路也都由假绿转为 dangling。真实 recursive-init 的二层 submodule untracked 覆盖完整，macOS `/tmp -> /private/tmp`、多级 symlink 和“经项目外中转但最终落回项目内”的正反例也符合预期。

+import importlib.util, os
+from pathlib import Path
+s = Path("harnessloop/plugins/harnessloop/skills/harnessloop-loop/scripts/verify_protocol.py")
+sp = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("vp", s)
+vp = importlib.util.module_from_spec(sp); sp.loader.exec_module(vp)
+p = Path(os.environ["T064_REPRO"])
+for label in ("before-force-add", "after-force-add"):
+    idx = vp.build_suffix_index(p)
+    hits = ["/".join(x) for x in idx.get("real.md", []) if x[-2:] == ("pkg", "real.md")]
+    print(label, hits, vp.suffix_unique_match("pkg/real.md", idx, p))
+    if label == "before-force-add":
+        import subprocess
+        subprocess.run(["git", "-C", str(p), "add", "-f", "scratch/pkg/real.md"], check=True)
+PY
+```
+
+实测为 `before-force-add [...] True`、`after-force-add [...] False`。同一文件系统事实仅因 index 状态翻转，正是 T-063 判为 MUST-FIX 的核心性质；把边界从 “untracked” 平移到 “ignored” 并未消除该假阴性。module docstring `verify_protocol.py:120-122` 诚实披露 ignored 文件不可见，但披露不等于闭合。
+
+#### MUST-FIX B：已删除 tracked 项不属于“真实存在”宇宙，却仍参与唯一性
+
+`git ls-files --cached`（`verify_protocol.py:590-600`）会列出工作树中已删除但尚未从 index 删除的 tracked 文件。实现只在“索引恰好唯一”后复验该路径（`:761-768`）；一旦一个不存在项与一个真实项同后缀，`:763-764` 会先以 `len(matches) != 1` 返回，根本没有过滤不存在项：
+
+```text
+src/pkg/real.md exists=true
+gone/pkg/real.md exists=false
+git ls-files --deleted=["gone/pkg/real.md"]
+index hits=["gone/pkg/real.md","src/pkg/real.md"]
+suffix_unique_match=false
+end_to_end dangling=["pkg/real.md"]
+```
+
+这不是假阴性，而是 TH-0008 所要减少的假阳性；也直接反驳 module docstring `:103-112` 和 `_git_tracked_index` docstring `:546-567` 的 “every file that genuinely exists in the worktree”。
+
+### 3. canonical containment — **普通组合正确；`symlink/..` 可绕**
+
+先验证要求中的正常组合：
+
+```text
+multi-level escape:
+  project/hop1 -> project/hop2 -> outside
+  hop1/pkg/out.md resolves outside
+  _resolve_in_project(...) = None
+
+outside intermediary, final target inside:
+  project/reenter -> outside/return -> project/inside
+  reenter/pkg/in.md resolves inside
+  _resolve_in_project(...) accepted
+  .gitmodules roots=["reenter"]  # escaping hop1 was dropped
+
+project root under symlink:
+  project argument=/tmp/.../project-alias
+  project.resolve()=/private/tmp/.../project-real
+  _is_contained(alias/pkg/x.md, alias)=true
+  _resolve_in_project(...)=accepted
+```
+
+原始三路无 `..` escape 也确实分别在 `verify_protocol.py:527`、`:766`、`:793` 调用 `_is_contained`，并分别返回拒绝；suffix 路径对项目外 file symlink 保持安全。
+
+#### MUST-FIX C：先词法折叠、后 canonicalize 改变了 symlink/`..` 的真实路径语义
+
+`_is_contained` 本身在 `verify_protocol.py:277-309` 正确对两侧 `resolve(strict=False)`；但 `_resolve_in_project` 在把 candidate 交给它之前，先于 `:792` 执行 `os.path.normpath(base / cited)`。`normpath` 会把 `link/..` 纯词法消掉，而真实文件系统应先跟随 `link` 再处理 `..`。
+
+最小复现：
+
+```bash
+python3 - <<'PY'
+import importlib.util, shutil, tempfile
+from pathlib import Path
+s = Path("harnessloop/plugins/harnessloop/skills/harnessloop-loop/scripts/verify_protocol.py")
+sp = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("vp", s)
+vp = importlib.util.module_from_spec(sp); sp.loader.exec_module(vp)
+r = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="t064-order-", dir="/tmp"))
+try:
+    p, out = r/"project", r/"outside"
+    (out/"sub").mkdir(parents=True); (out/"escape.md").write_text("outside")
+    p.mkdir(); (p/"escape.md").write_text("coincidental inside")
+    (p/"link").symlink_to(out/"sub", target_is_directory=True)
+    raw = p/"link/../escape.md"
+    got = vp._resolve_in_project(p, "link/../escape.md", p)
+    print("raw_resolves_to", raw.resolve())
+    print("raw_contained", vp._is_contained(raw, p))
+    print("helper_returns", got)
+    print("helper_target", got.resolve() if got else None)
+finally:
+    shutil.rmtree(r)
+PY
+```
+
+实测：
+
+```text
+raw_resolves_to /private/tmp/.../outside/escape.md
+raw_contained False
+helper_returns /tmp/.../project/escape.md
+helper_target /private/tmp/.../project/escape.md
+```
+
+把引用 `link/../escape.md` 放进 review 后，v0.14 与 v0.15 均 `dangling=[]`。也就是说 v0.15 没有 containment-check 原始 candidate，而是先把它改写成另一个项目内同名文件后再检查。
+
+`.gitmodules` 有同一顺序错误：`submodule_roots` 先在 `verify_protocol.py:525-527` 对 candidate 做 `normpath` 后才 containment-check，却在 `:529-530` 对未折叠的原 candidate 做 `is_dir()` 并返回它。fixture：
+
+```text
+project/smod -> outside/sub
+.gitmodules path = smod/../mod
+raw .gitmodules path resolves to outside/mod
+_is_contained(raw_path, project)=false
+submodule_roots nevertheless returns [".../project/smod/../mod"]
+
+project/mod/pkg/modghost.md exists
+project/other/pkg/modghost.md exists
+suffix hits=2
+suffix_unique_match=false
+review cites pkg/modghost.md
+end_to_end dangling=[]   # 只能是错误接纳的 .gitmodules base 使其假绿
+```
+
+因此直接 base 与 `.gitmodules` base 都可独立绕过；suffix hit 这一条仍安全，但“三条路径统一受 canonical containment 保护”的整体声明不成立。
+
+### 4. 诚实性 — **索引边界与固有残留诚实；canonical 与“真实存在”两处过强**
+
+- canonical module docstring `verify_protocol.py:52-64` 声称 candidate 会直接经 `Path.resolve(strict=False)` 且项目外 symlink/`..` “never treated as resolved”；实际 `:792` 与 `:525-527` 会先词法折叠，以上反例假绿。`--help` 在 `:982-990` 同步了相同的绝对声明，因此同步了文字，但没有同步到真实能力。
+- 索引关于 “tracked + untracked-not-ignored” 的边界描述与查询一致（module docstring `:103-130`，helper docstring `:546-567`，`--help :987-990`），ignored 不可见也在 `:120-122` 明说。可是“every file that genuinely exists” 不准确：`:590-600` 纳入 deleted tracked，且多命中时不会做存在性过滤。
+- 非 Git 回退仍按 `:128-130,662-689` 的文字剪枝，实测一致。
+- 两条既有固有残留未被悄悄改称已修，登记仍在 `:163-185`。行为复验：
+
+```text
+only vendor/mistyped/config.yaml exists
+suffix_unique_match("mistyped/config.yaml")=true
+
+actual Case/File.md; citation case/file.md on this macOS volume
+Path.exists()=true
+_any_base_resolves(...)=true
+```
+
+后者正体现文档所述宿主大小写依赖，前者正体现后缀语义碰撞；两项均仍诚实。
+
+## Decisions / deviations
+
+- Verdict 使用任务专用枚举 `MUST-FIX`，而不是通用模板的 `REWORK`。
+- 判定口径：gitignored 文件虽可被排除为正向解析目标，但只要它真实存在并与一个可解析候选同后缀，就至少应阻止 suffix fallback 声称“唯一”；否则 `git add -f` 仍会翻转同一 worktree 事实。这是沿用 T-063 的保守假阴性口径。
+- T-063 原始两例与扩展反例分开结论：不抹杀原始修复已经生效，也不以原始守卫全绿代替组合审计。
+- 未修改被评审源码；全部临时 fixture 已清理。
+
+## Open questions
+
+none
+
+## Verdict
+
+**MUST-FIX**
+
+## Next recommendation
+
+先修 canonical 顺序：`_resolve_in_project` 与 `submodule_roots` 都应对未经词法折叠的 `base / cited`、`project / rel` 做 canonical containment，并让后续存在性检查沿用同一原始路径语义；新增 `symlink/../file` 的直接 base 与 `.gitmodules path` 两个端到端红测。索引侧应在判唯一之前过滤当前不存在的 tracked 项，并把 ignored 同后缀项至少作为 ambiguity-only 候选（不可用于正向 resolved，但可阻止伪唯一），分别加入 ignored-before/after-`git add -f` 稳定性和 deleted-tracked+one-real 回归守卫。完成后重跑 T-063 原始两例、上述三条新反例、真实 recursive submodule/NUL fixture 与全量 `HARNESSLOOP_SKIP_CLAUDE=1 python3 scripts/validate.py`，再确认 TH-0008 fixed。

+# T-064 · harnessloop v0.15.0 / T-063 MUST-FIX 确认审
+
+## Summary
+
+`1153b7f` 对 T-063 两个原始反例的字面复现均已闭合：untracked 冲突在 `git add` 前后都保持 dangling，普通 symlink 出界的直接 base、`.gitmodules` base、suffix hit 三路也都由假绿转为 dangling。真实 recursive-init 的二层 submodule untracked 覆盖完整，macOS `/tmp -> /private/tmp`、多级 symlink 和“经项目外中转但最终落回项目内”的正反例也符合预期。
+
+但不能确认 TH-0008 fixed：gitignored 的真实冲突仍制造与 T-063 同形、且会被 `git add -f` 翻转的伪唯一；canonical containment 在 `symlink/..` 组合上因先 `normpath` 后 `resolve` 而把真实项目外路径改写为项目内同名路径，直接 base 与 `.gitmodules` base 均可端到端假绿。此外，`--cached` 把已从工作树删除的 tracked 项仍计入“真实存在”宇宙，制造新的假阳性并使索引 docstring 不实。
+
+## Files touched
+
+- `.hopper/handoffs/T-064-output.md`：本只读确认审交付物。
+- 被评审的 `harnessloop` 源码：none；所有 Git/submodule/symlink fixture 均建于 `/tmp` 并已删除，结束时 `git -C harnessloop status --short` 无输出。
+
+## Acceptance verification (4/4)
+
+### 1. 两条 T-063 MUST-FIX 原始反例 — **字面反例均闭合**
+
+从 `git show 1153b7f^:plugins/harnessloop/skills/harnessloop-loop/scripts/verify_protocol.py` 加载 v0.14.0，从 `git show 1153b7f:...` 加载 v0.15.0，原样复跑 `.hopper/handoffs/T-063-output.md` 第 2、3 节 fixture，结果：
+
+```text
+untracked_pseudo_unique:
+  before git add scratch:
+    v0.14 dangling=[]
+    v0.15 dangling=["pkg/real.md"]
+    v0.15 suffix hits=["src/pkg/real.md","scratch/pkg/real.md"]
+  after git add scratch:
+    v0.14 dangling=["pkg/real.md"]
+    v0.15 dangling=["pkg/real.md"]
+
+symlink_containment_escape:
+  v0.14 helpers:
+    direct_candidate_accepted=true
+    submodule_roots=[".../project/link"]
+    suffix_unique_match=true
+    end_to_end_dangling=[]
+  v0.15 helpers:
+    direct_candidate_accepted=false
+    submodule_roots=[]
+    suffix_unique_match=false
+    end_to_end_dangling=[
+      "link/pkg/ghost.py",
+      "pkg/ghost.py",
+      "pkg/external.md"
+    ]
+  mutation targets:
+    .../outside/pkg/ghost.py
+    .../outside/pkg/ghost.py
+```
+
+对应实现调用点为 `verify_protocol.py:527`（`.gitmodules`）、`:765-768`（suffix hit）、`:792-795`（直接/任意 base）。新增官方守卫位于 `scripts/validate.py:1286-1539`，且基线全绿：
+
+```text
+$ HARNESSLOOP_SKIP_CLAUDE=1 python3 scripts/validate.py
+...
+ok: verify reports `pkg/real.md` as dangling ... untracked_pseudo_unique
+ok: _resolve_in_project rejects ... direct base
+ok: submodule_roots rejects ... .gitmodules base
+ok: suffix_unique_match rejects ... suffix fallback
+Plugin framework validation passed.
+```
+
+因此，“T-063 原始两个最小反例是否由修前假绿变为修后报错”这一窄问题答案是 **是**。
+
+### 2. 新唯一性宇宙 — **普通 untracked 与二层 submodule 正确；仍有 ignored 伪唯一和 stale-tracked 假阳性**
+
+实现确实使用三路 NUL 查询：tracked 为 `git ls-files -z --cached --recurse-submodules`（`verify_protocol.py:589-600`），顶层 untracked 为 `git ls-files -z --others --exclude-standard`（`:602-611`），已 checkout submodule 的 untracked 为 `git submodule foreach --recursive`（`:613-632`），最后按完整相对路径去重（`:634-648`）。
+
+真实 `outer -> modules/middle -> deps/leaf` fixture 执行 `git -c protocol.file.allow=always submodule update --init --recursive` 后：
+
+```text
+recursive_init_rc=0
+foreach:
+  modules/middle|modules/middle
+  deps/leaf|modules/middle/deps/leaf
+tracked pkg/tracked.md:
+  modules/middle/deps/leaf/deep/pkg/tracked.md
+  modules/middle/mid/pkg/tracked.md
+  top/pkg/tracked.md
+untracked:
+  modules/middle/fresh/pkg/middle.md
+  modules/middle/deps/leaf/fresh/pkg/leaf.md
+newline filename:
+  "modules/middle/deps/leaf/fresh/pkg/line\nbreak.md"
+ignored.md hits=[]
+```
+
+所以 recursive-init 后的嵌套 tracked/untracked 覆盖完整，NUL 边界也通过换行文件名实测；先前未 init 时 leaf 文件本身不在 worktree，不能算漏索引。
+
+非 Git 回退也仍与文档一致：
+
+```text
+_git_tracked_index(non_git_project) = None
+src/pkg/real.md exists
+build/pkg/real.md exists
+indexed hits=["src/pkg/real.md"]
+suffix_unique_match("pkg/real.md")=true
+```
+
+即 `os.walk` 在 `verify_protocol.py:681-689` 继续剪掉 `NOISE_DIR_NAMES`；module docstring `:128-130` 和 `build_suffix_index` docstring `:662-675` 如实登记了这个旧盲点。
+
+#### MUST-FIX A：gitignored 真实冲突仍是同形伪唯一
+
+`--exclude-standard` 在 `verify_protocol.py:603-605` 明确排除 ignored 文件。构造 tracked `src/pkg/real.md`、真实且 ignored 的 `scratch/pkg/real.md` 与引用 `pkg/real.md`：
+
+```text
+git check-ignore scratch/pkg/real.md -> rc 0
+before git add -f:
+  suffix hits=["src/pkg/real.md"]
+  dangling=[]
+after git add -f scratch/pkg/real.md:
+  suffix hits=["scratch/pkg/real.md","src/pkg/real.md"]
+  dangling=["pkg/real.md"]
+```

+### 3. canonical containment — **普通组合正确；`symlink/..` 可绕**
+
+先验证要求中的正常组合：

+```text

+  hop1/pkg/out.md resolves outside
+  _resolve_in_project(...) = None

+```
+

+
+#### MUST-FIX C：先词法折叠、后 canonicalize 改变了 symlink/`..` 的真实路径语义
+
+`_is_contained` 本身在 `verify_protocol.py:277-309` 正确对两侧 `resolve(strict=False)`；但 `_resolve_in_project` 在把 candidate 交给它之前，先于 `:792` 执行 `os.path.normpath(base / cited)`。`normpath` 会把 `link/..` 纯词法消掉，而真实文件系统应先跟随 `link` 再处理 `..`。
+
+最小复现：
+
+```bash
+python3 - <<'PY'
+import importlib.util, shutil, tempfile
+from pathlib import Path
+s = Path("harnessloop/plugins/harnessloop/skills/harnessloop-loop/scripts/verify_protocol.py")
+sp = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("vp", s)
+vp = importlib.util.module_from_spec(sp); sp.loader.exec_module(vp)
+r = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="t064-order-", dir="/tmp"))
+try:
+    p, out = r/"project", r/"outside"
+    (out/"sub").mkdir(parents=True); (out/"escape.md").write_text("outside")
+    p.mkdir(); (p/"escape.md").write_text("coincidental inside")
+    (p/"link").symlink_to(out/"sub", target_is_directory=True)
+    raw = p/"link/../escape.md"
+    got = vp._resolve_in_project(p, "link/../escape.md", p)
+    print("raw_resolves_to", raw.resolve())
+    print("raw_contained", vp._is_contained(raw, p))
+    print("helper_returns", got)
+    print("helper_target", got.resolve() if got else None)
+finally:
+    shutil.rmtree(r)
+PY
+```
+
+实测：
+
+```text
+raw_resolves_to /private/tmp/.../outside/escape.md
+raw_contained False
+helper_returns /tmp/.../project/escape.md
+helper_target /private/tmp/.../project/escape.md
+```
+
+把引用 `link/../escape.md` 放进 review 后，v0.14 与 v0.15 均 `dangling=[]`。也就是说 v0.15 没有 containment-check 原始 candidate，而是先把它改写成另一个项目内同名文件后再检查。
+
+`.gitmodules` 有同一顺序错误：`submodule_roots` 先在 `verify_protocol.py:525-527` 对 candidate 做 `normpath` 后才 containment-check，却在 `:529-530` 对未折叠的原 candidate 做 `is_dir()` 并返回它。fixture：
+
+```text
+project/smod -> outside/sub
+.gitmodules path = smod/../mod
+raw .gitmodules path resolves to outside/mod
+_is_contained(raw_path, project)=false
+submodule_roots nevertheless returns [".../project/smod/../mod"]
+
+project/mod/pkg/modghost.md exists
+project/other/pkg/modghost.md exists
+suffix hits=2
+suffix_unique_match=false
+review cites pkg/modghost.md
+end_to_end dangling=[]   # 只能是错误接纳的 .gitmodules base 使其假绿
+```
+
+因此直接 base 与 `.gitmodules` base 都可独立绕过；suffix hit 这一条仍安全，但“三条路径统一受 canonical containment 保护”的整体声明不成立。
+
+### 4. 诚实性 — **索引边界与固有残留诚实；canonical 与“真实存在”两处过强**
+
+- canonical module docstring `verify_protocol.py:52-64` 声称 candidate 会直接经 `Path.resolve(strict=False)` 且项目外 symlink/`..` “never treated as resolved”；实际 `:792` 与 `:525-527` 会先词法折叠，以上反例假绿。`--help` 在 `:982-990` 同步了相同的绝对声明，因此同步了文字，但没有同步到真实能力。
+- 索引关于 “tracked + untracked-not-ignored” 的边界描述与查询一致（module docstring `:103-130`，helper docstring `:546-567`，`--help :987-990`），ignored 不可见也在 `:120-122` 明说。可是“every file that genuinely exists” 不准确：`:590-600` 纳入 deleted tracked，且多命中时不会做存在性过滤。
+- 非 Git 回退仍按 `:128-130,662-689` 的文字剪枝，实测一致。
+- 两条既有固有残留未被悄悄改称已修，登记仍在 `:163-185`。行为复验：
+
+```text
+only vendor/mistyped/config.yaml exists
+suffix_unique_match("mistyped/config.yaml")=true
+
+actual Case/File.md; citation case/file.md on this macOS volume
+Path.exists()=true
+_any_base_resolves(...)=true
+```
+
+后者正体现文档所述宿主大小写依赖，前者正体现后缀语义碰撞；两项均仍诚实。
+
+## Decisions / deviations
+
+- Verdict 使用任务专用枚举 `MUST-FIX`，而不是通用模板的 `REWORK`。
+- 判定口径：gitignored 文件虽可被排除为正向解析目标，但只要它真实存在并与一个可解析候选同后缀，就至少应阻止 suffix fallback 声称“唯一”；否则 `git add -f` 仍会翻转同一 worktree 事实。这是沿用 T-063 的保守假阴性口径。
+- T-063 原始两例与扩展反例分开结论：不抹杀原始修复已经生效，也不以原始守卫全绿代替组合审计。
+- 未修改被评审源码；全部临时 fixture 已清理。

+# T-064 · harnessloop v0.15.0 / T-063 MUST-FIX 确认审
+
+## Summary
+
+`1153b7f` 对 T-063 两个原始反例的字面复现均已闭合：untracked 冲突在 `git add` 前后都保持 dangling，普通 symlink 出界的直接 base、`.gitmodules` base、suffix hit 三路也都由假绿转为 dangling。真实 recursive-init 的二层 submodule untracked 覆盖完整，macOS `/tmp -> /private/tmp`、多级 symlink 和“经项目外中转但最终落回项目内”的正反例也符合预期。
+
+但不能确认 TH-0008 fixed：gitignored 的真实冲突仍制造与 T-063 同形、且会被 `git add -f` 翻转的伪唯一；canonical containment 在 `symlink/..` 组合上因先 `normpath` 后 `resolve` 而把真实项目外路径改写为项目内同名路径，直接 base 与 `.gitmodules` base 均可端到端假绿。此外，`--cached` 把已从工作树删除的 tracked 项仍计入“真实存在”宇宙，制造新的假阳性并使索引 docstring 不实。
+

+- `.hopper/handoffs/T-064-output.md`：本只读确认审交付物。

+## Acceptance verification (4/4)
+
+### 1. 两条 T-063 MUST-FIX 原始反例 — **字面反例均闭合**
+
+从 `git show 1153b7f^:plugins/harnessloop/skills/harnessloop-loop/scripts/verify_protocol.py` 加载 v0.14.0，从 `git show 1153b7f:...` 加载 v0.15.0，原样复跑 `.hopper/handoffs/T-063-output.md` 第 2、3 节 fixture，结果：
+
+```text
+untracked_pseudo_unique:
+  before git add scratch:
+    v0.14 dangling=[]
+    v0.15 dangling=["pkg/real.md"]
+    v0.15 suffix hits=["src/pkg/real.md","scratch/pkg/real.md"]
+  after git add scratch:
+    v0.14 dangling=["pkg/real.md"]
+    v0.15 dangling=["pkg/real.md"]
+
+symlink_containment_escape:
+  v0.14 helpers:
+    direct_candidate_accepted=true
+    submodule_roots=[".../project/link"]
+    suffix_unique_match=true
+    end_to_end_dangling=[]
+  v0.15 helpers:

+
+对应实现调用点为 `verify_protocol.py:527`（`.gitmodules`）、`:765-768`（suffix hit）、`:792-795`（直接/任意 base）。新增官方守卫位于 `scripts/validate.py:1286-1539`，且基线全绿：
+
+```text
+$ HARNESSLOOP_SKIP_CLAUDE=1 python3 scripts/validate.py
+...
+ok: verify reports `pkg/real.md` as dangling ... untracked_pseudo_unique
+ok: _resolve_in_project rejects ... direct base
+ok: submodule_roots rejects ... .gitmodules base
+ok: suffix_unique_match rejects ... suffix fallback
+Plugin framework validation passed.
+```
+
+因此，“T-063 原始两个最小反例是否由修前假绿变为修后报错”这一窄问题答案是 **是**。
+
+### 2. 新唯一性宇宙 — **普通 untracked 与二层 submodule 正确；仍有 ignored 伪唯一和 stale-tracked 假阳性**
+
+实现确实使用三路 NUL 查询：tracked 为 `git ls-files -z --cached --recurse-submodules`（`verify_protocol.py:589-600`），顶层 untracked 为 `git ls-files -z --others --exclude-standard`（`:602-611`），已 checkout submodule 的 untracked 为 `git submodule foreach --recursive`（`:613-632`），最后按完整相对路径去重（`:634-648`）。
+
+真实 `outer -> modules/middle -> deps/leaf` fixture 执行 `git -c protocol.file.allow=always submodule update --init --recursive` 后：
+
+```text
+recursive_init_rc=0
+foreach:
+  modules/middle|modules/middle
+  deps/leaf|modules/middle/deps/leaf
+tracked pkg/tracked.md:
+  modules/middle/deps/leaf/deep/pkg/tracked.md
+  modules/middle/mid/pkg/tracked.md
+  top/pkg/tracked.md
+untracked:
+  modules/middle/fresh/pkg/middle.md
+  modules/middle/deps/leaf/fresh/pkg/leaf.md
+newline filename:
+  "modules/middle/deps/leaf/fresh/pkg/line\nbreak.md"
+ignored.md hits=[]
+```
+
+所以 recursive-init 后的嵌套 tracked/untracked 覆盖完整，NUL 边界也通过换行文件名实测；先前未 init 时 leaf 文件本身不在 worktree，不能算漏索引。
+
+非 Git 回退也仍与文档一致：
+
+```text
+_git_tracked_index(non_git_project) = None
+src/pkg/real.md exists
+build/pkg/real.md exists
+indexed hits=["src/pkg/real.md"]
+suffix_unique_match("pkg/real.md")=true
+```
+
+即 `os.walk` 在 `verify_protocol.py:681-689` 继续剪掉 `NOISE_DIR_NAMES`；module docstring `:128-130` 和 `build_suffix_index` docstring `:662-675` 如实登记了这个旧盲点。
+
+#### MUST-FIX A：gitignored 真实冲突仍是同形伪唯一
+
+`--exclude-standard` 在 `verify_protocol.py:603-605` 明确排除 ignored 文件。构造 tracked `src/pkg/real.md`、真实且 ignored 的 `scratch/pkg/real.md` 与引用 `pkg/real.md`：
+
+```text
+git check-ignore scratch/pkg/real.md -> rc 0
+before git add -f:
+  suffix hits=["src/pkg/real.md"]
+  dangling=[]
+after git add -f scratch/pkg/real.md:
+  suffix hits=["scratch/pkg/real.md","src/pkg/real.md"]
+  dangling=["pkg/real.md"]
+```
 
-# T-064 — codex (background, in-progress)
+复现步骤：
 
-Output streaming to `T-064-output.log`. Status updates here.
+```bash
+T064_REPRO="$(mktemp -d /tmp/t064-ignore.XXXXXX)"
+git -C "$T064_REPRO" init -q
+mkdir -p "$T064_REPRO/src/pkg" "$T064_REPRO/scratch/pkg"

+printf ignored >"$T064_REPRO/scratch/pkg/real.md"

+```
+
+实测为 `before-force-add [...] True`、`after-force-add [...] False`。同一文件系统事实仅因 index 状态翻转，正是 T-063 判为 MUST-FIX 的核心性质；把边界从 “untracked” 平移到 “ignored” 并未消除该假阴性。module docstring `verify_protocol.py:120-122` 诚实披露 ignored 文件不可见，但披露不等于闭合。
+
+#### MUST-FIX B：已删除 tracked 项不属于“真实存在”宇宙，却仍参与唯一性
+
+`git ls-files --cached`（`verify_protocol.py:590-600`）会列出工作树中已删除但尚未从 index 删除的 tracked 文件。实现只在“索引恰好唯一”后复验该路径（`:761-768`）；一旦一个不存在项与一个真实项同后缀，`:763-764` 会先以 `len(matches) != 1` 返回，根本没有过滤不存在项：
+
+```text
+src/pkg/real.md exists=true
+gone/pkg/real.md exists=false
+git ls-files --deleted=["gone/pkg/real.md"]
+index hits=["gone/pkg/real.md","src/pkg/real.md"]
+suffix_unique_match=false
+end_to_end dangling=["pkg/real.md"]
+```
+
+这不是假阴性，而是 TH-0008 所要减少的假阳性；也直接反驳 module docstring `:103-112` 和 `_git_tracked_index` docstring `:546-567` 的 “every file that genuinely exists in the worktree”。
+
+### 3. canonical containment — **普通组合正确；`symlink/..` 可绕**
+
+先验证要求中的正常组合：
+
+```text
+multi-level escape:
+  project/hop1 -> project/hop2 -> outside
+  hop1/pkg/out.md resolves outside
+  _resolve_in_project(...) = None
+
+outside intermediary, final target inside:
+  project/reenter -> outside/return -> project/inside
+  reenter/pkg/in.md resolves inside
+  _resolve_in_project(...) accepted
+  .gitmodules roots=["reenter"]  # escaping hop1 was dropped
+
+project root under symlink:
+  project argument=/tmp/.../project-alias
+  project.resolve()=/private/tmp/.../project-real
+  _is_contained(alias/pkg/x.md, alias)=true
+  _resolve_in_project(...)=accepted
+```
+
+原始三路无 `..` escape 也确实分别在 `verify_protocol.py:527`、`:766`、`:793` 调用 `_is_contained`，并分别返回拒绝；suffix 路径对项目外 file symlink 保持安全。
+
+#### MUST-FIX C：先词法折叠、后 canonicalize 改变了 symlink/`..` 的真实路径语义
+
+`_is_contained` 本身在 `verify_protocol.py:277-309` 正确对两侧 `resolve(strict=False)`；但 `_resolve_in_project` 在把 candidate 交给它之前，先于 `:792` 执行 `os.path.normpath(base / cited)`。`normpath` 会把 `link/..` 纯词法消掉，而真实文件系统应先跟随 `link` 再处理 `..`。
+
+最小复现：
+
+```bash
+python3 - <<'PY'
+import importlib.util, shutil, tempfile
+from pathlib import Path
+s = Path("harnessloop/plugins/harnessloop/skills/harnessloop-loop/scripts/verify_protocol.py")
+sp = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("vp", s)
+vp = importlib.util.module_from_spec(sp); sp.loader.exec_module(vp)
+r = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="t064-order-", dir="/tmp"))
+try:
+    p, out = r/"project", r/"outside"
+    (out/"sub").mkdir(parents=True); (out/"escape.md").write_text("outside")
+    p.mkdir(); (p/"escape.md").write_text("coincidental inside")
+    (p/"link").symlink_to(out/"sub", target_is_directory=True)

+    got = vp._resolve_in_project(p, "link/../escape.md", p)
+    print("raw_resolves_to", raw.resolve())
+    print("raw_contained", vp._is_contained(raw, p))
+    print("helper_returns", got)
+    print("helper_target", got.resolve() if got else None)
+finally:
+    shutil.rmtree(r)
+PY
+```
+

+raw_contained False
+helper_returns /tmp/.../project/escape.md
+helper_target /private/tmp/.../project/escape.md
+```
+
+把引用 `link/../escape.md` 放进 review 后，v0.14 与 v0.15 均 `dangling=[]`。也就是说 v0.15 没有 containment-check 原始 candidate，而是先把它改写成另一个项目内同名文件后再检查。

+- Verdict 使用任务专用枚举 `MUST-FIX`，而不是通用模板的 `REWORK`。
+- 判定口径：gitignored 文件虽可被排除为正向解析目标，但只要它真实存在并与一个可解析候选同后缀，就至少应阻止 suffix fallback 声称“唯一”；否则 `git add -f` 仍会翻转同一 worktree 事实。这是沿用 T-063 的保守假阴性口径。
+- T-063 原始两例与扩展反例分开结论：不抹杀原始修复已经生效，也不以原始守卫全绿代替组合审计。
+- 未修改被评审源码；全部临时 fixture 已清理。
